1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any separation module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 46. The projective resolution has length at most 462 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to separation theory, we decompose the problem into 46 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 462. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the separation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 969 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 746. QED.
